# Citebench corpus manifest

Freeze date: **2026-09-10**

## Policy

Freely redistributable public documentation only: Kubernetes docs (CC BY 4.0),
Microsoft Learn Azure docs (CC BY 4.0), the PostgreSQL manual (PostgreSQL
License), FastAPI docs (MIT), and the MIT-licensed xv6 book (PDF). No internal
runbooks, no paywalled pages, no pirated books, no customer data. Ingest snapshots each pinned URL to
`corpus/raw/<id>/` (HTML or PDF) with a companion `sha256` file. Do not add
documents that are not in this table. The small, immutable smoke corpus is
`corpus/v0/`.

## Re-freeze rule

A re-freeze requires all of: a new freeze date, a complete replacement of the
pinned URL list below, license re-check, and a `docs/decision-trail.tsv` row.
Do not silently swap a URL or version.
